# Compatibility matrix

Generated by `python3 tools/compatibility-report.py` from the tracked JSON inventory
and current source. Status is deliberately conservative: parser recognition or an
owning test file does not prove the complete requirement. Edit the JSON evidence
when closing requirements, then regenerate. Local PLAN.md/PLAN2.md are not needed.

Supported = accepted documented behavior; partial = some owning implementation;
planned = no accepted implementation; excluded = explicit recorded scope decision.

[Release scope and decisions](release-scope.md) · [Build baseline](build-baseline.md)

full requirement acceptance is still open. QTEMP namespace checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-26 runtime-owned private libraries initialize atomically with jobs and for existing live jobs after upgrade. Catalog, user spaces, typed data areas, STD queues, inline CL program objects, authority and allocations resolve the executing job; even privileged foreign-job backing-name access is denied. Native return names stay QTEMP. Submission/groups receive separate namespaces. Completion/cancellation and exclusive-owner crash recovery clean payloads and bindings atomically, preserving unclaimed queued jobs. Private files and other specialized stores, generic user-space deletion, MI/list behavior and full lock/error/domain relocation fidelity remain open; broad C11/C14 items remain unchecked. Final QTEMP verification (2026-10-17): all 1689 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 25 new namespace/storage/ABI/security/process/server cases and eight additional migration-version cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with QTEMP user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Job-history deletion acceptance now completes jobs before purging their rows, preserving namespace cleanup and non-reused job numbers. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. QTEMP file checkpoint (2026-10-17): the file store resolves the owning namespace before definitions, members, source revisions, record CRUD/keyed/sequential cursors, copy and logical paths. Private source/PF/simple LF members now compile through CRTCLPGM/CRTBNDRPG/CRTPF, with CL RCVF and RPG shared ODP consumption. Permanent LFs cannot capture private PF storage; private LF unique paths on global PFs are released or retained according to other owners. Process writers, exact decimals, two CCSIDs, source revision conflicts, privileged cross-job denial, DDL/cleanup rollback, parsed dependencies, reopen/recovery and real terminal source/data lifecycle are covered. Other private domain stores and broader C08/C11/C14 contracts remain open; checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. Final QTEMP file verification (2026-10-17): all 1710 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 21 new private file/source/member/ABI/process/recovery cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with private source copy/DDS+CL compile/read/display/delete, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. The process fixture lock updates add project references only; package versions are unchanged. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

Native date API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCCVTDT now accepts complete 5/10/11-argument groups and native short/long calendar/Julian date-time layouts, CURRENT/SYSVAL/DTS and DOS formats. The shared timestamp codec preserves standard 52-bit microsecond/12-bit uniqueness semantics; the API applies its eight-microsecond conversion quantum and native millisecond rounding/ranges/wrap rules. Character conversion preserves unvalidated time bytes and short century copy behavior. UTC/actual host-zone conversion validates DST gaps, supports both repeated-time segments and rounds on the UTC instant across transitions. UTC/out-of-range information receivers use zero binary/blank character fields and preserve tails. Compiled CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208, live program authority/signing/manifests, output preparation/cancellation and real terminal flows have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects and in-range host-zone metadata are explicitly unsupported pending shared services. Wider C14 API/crypto requirements remain open: 35 complete / 71 open. See docs/date-time-apis.md for exact layouts, selectors and errors. Final native date API verification (2026-10-17): all 2240 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 new timestamp/layout/range/copy/DST/DOS/control/error/cancellation and CL/RPG integration cases. All 74 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG microsecond timestamp round-trips and WRKAPILS details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published MI vectors verify the shared full-precision codec; QWCCVTDT conversion applies an eight-microsecond quantum inferred from IBM precision notes/endpoints. Native copy rules preserve raw time/century bytes where validation is not required. Rounding across DST and New Year, invalid local times, both repeated-time segments and atomic output preparation are verified. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects, in-range host-zone metadata and wider API/crypto families remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

[tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoContextTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoContextTests.cs); [tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs](../tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs) | 2026-09-14: fresh/legacy upgrades, migration rollback, backup restore, concurrent processes, version/history rejection; migration test suite passed. Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native cryptography checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS QC3 adapters now provide hash/HMAC, AES encrypt/decrypt and opaque job-owned key contexts through shared .NET services. Five hashes, three AES key sizes, ECB/CBC/CTR, native padding, checked DATA0100/ALGD0500/ALGD0200/KEYD0100/0200 descriptors and atomic output/ length/error preparation have published known-answer acceptance. Schema 29 stores keys under authenticated AES-GCM envelopes, preserves existing catalog data/IDs, checks current job/profile ownership, limits contexts per job, supports wrapping inventory/rotation and deletes contexts with completion/crash recovery. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and real PTY run all six programs; independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST now constructs raw descriptors with checked bounds, exact byte tails and literal-text preservation. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters. Streaming algorithm contexts, persistent native keystores, additional cipher/format families and ILE binding retain their broader open requirements. See docs/cryptography-apis.md. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

[tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/NativeJobIdentityTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/NativeJobIdentityTests.cs) | Schema 15: shared typed-job lifecycle, cancellation/shutdown, activation storage, managed/native accounting, authorized work commands and queue bindings; adapter-specific networking and full spool/message semantics remain in their owning packages. C11 private job-area storage acceptance (2026-10-17): automatic CHAR LDA creation and atomic submission copies, shared GDA/group lifetime, and the previously documented C04 raw routing-PIP extension are audited complete. 27 additional cases cover seven CCSIDs, exact bounds/detached reads, queued restart, failed-claim rollback, pending-payload clearing, job-identity denial, process concurrency and cancellation/cleanup. Missing GDA/PDA return CPF1046/CPF1072 through MONMSG. Full terminal group/secondary transfers preserve shared GDA, independent LDAs/groups and edited panels. Native prestart protocols and broader work/API/typed-data-area requirements remain open. Final job-area verification: all 1599 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 27 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with group data-area acceptance, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QTEMP namespace checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-26 runtime-owned private libraries initialize atomically with jobs and for existing live jobs after upgrade. Catalog, user spaces, typed data areas, STD queues, inline CL program objects, authority and allocations resolve the executing job; even privileged foreign-job backing-name access is denied. Native return names stay QTEMP. Submission/groups receive separate namespaces. Completion/cancellation and exclusive-owner crash recovery clean payloads and bindings atomically, preserving unclaimed queued jobs. Private files and other specialized stores, generic user-space deletion, MI/list behavior and full lock/error/domain relocation fidelity remain open; broad C11/C14 items remain unchecked. Final QTEMP verification (2026-10-17): all 1689 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 25 new namespace/storage/ABI/security/process/server cases and eight additional migration-version cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with QTEMP user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Job-history deletion acceptance now completes jobs before purging their rows, preserving namespace cleanup and non-reused job numbers. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. QTEMP file checkpoint (2026-10-17): the file store resolves the owning namespace before definitions, members, source revisions, record CRUD/keyed/sequential cursors, copy and logical paths. Private source/PF/simple LF members now compile through CRTCLPGM/CRTBNDRPG/CRTPF, with CL RCVF and RPG shared ODP consumption. Permanent LFs cannot capture private PF storage; private LF unique paths on global PFs are released or retained according to other owners. Process writers, exact decimals, two CCSIDs, source revision conflicts, privileged cross-job denial, DDL/cleanup rollback, parsed dependencies, reopen/recovery and real terminal source/data lifecycle are covered. Other private domain stores and broader C08/C11/C14 contracts remain open; checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. Final QTEMP file verification (2026-10-17): all 1710 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 21 new private file/source/member/ABI/process/recovery cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with private source copy/DDS+CL compile/read/display/delete, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. The process fixture lock updates add project references only; package versions are unchanged. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

[tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/SessionServerTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/SessionServerTests.cs) | Owning code/test files only; full requirement acceptance is still open. C11 private job-area storage acceptance (2026-10-17): automatic CHAR LDA creation and atomic submission copies, shared GDA/group lifetime, and the previously documented C04 raw routing-PIP extension are audited complete. 27 additional cases cover seven CCSIDs, exact bounds/detached reads, queued restart, failed-claim rollback, pending-payload clearing, job-identity denial, process concurrency and cancellation/cleanup. Missing GDA/PDA return CPF1046/CPF1072 through MONMSG. Full terminal group/secondary transfers preserve shared GDA, independent LDAs/groups and edited panels. Native prestart protocols and broader work/API/typed-data-area requirements remain open. Final job-area verification: all 1599 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 27 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with group data-area acceptance, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QTEMP namespace checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-26 runtime-owned private libraries initialize atomically with jobs and for existing live jobs after upgrade. Catalog, user spaces, typed data areas, STD queues, inline CL program objects, authority and allocations resolve the executing job; even privileged foreign-job backing-name access is denied. Native return names stay QTEMP. Submission/groups receive separate namespaces. Completion/cancellation and exclusive-owner crash recovery clean payloads and bindings atomically, preserving unclaimed queued jobs. Private files and other specialized stores, generic user-space deletion, MI/list behavior and full lock/error/domain relocation fidelity remain open; broad C11/C14 items remain unchecked. Final QTEMP verification (2026-10-17): all 1689 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 25 new namespace/storage/ABI/security/process/server cases and eight additional migration-version cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with QTEMP user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Job-history deletion acceptance now completes jobs before purging their rows, preserving namespace cleanup and non-reused job numbers. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

Message-subfile binding checkpoint (2026-10-17): live job/frame queue sources resolve output callers, external queues and nearest recursive programs without foreign-job access. Row writes capture text/help; SFLINZ reads a single bounded queue snapshot and grows the subfile beyond SFLSIZ. Display reads leave seen, exception, inquiry and reply state unchanged. Paging/help survive queue entry and private MSGF removal; failed initialization/output restores rows, capacity, page and editor. Twenty-four new tests and a live terminal fixture cover opaque keys, two CCSIDs, 80/132-column output, more than 1000 messages, authority and DDS diagnostics. CL SNDRCVF, RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT, record/RRN bindings, terminal suspension and ILE 276-byte queue names remain open; broad C07/C09/C11 checklist items stay unchecked. Final message-subfile verification (2026-10-17): all 1863 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 24 new message-subfile cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including live SFLINZ/paging/saved second-level help after source removal, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; CL/RPG terminal exchange and broader C07/C09/C11 work remain unfinished. RPG workstation checkpoint (2026-10-17): native/fixed F specs and free DCL-F/DCL-S declarations bind saved DSPF layouts to local/socket EXFMT and separate READ/WRITE. SFILE RRNs, subfile WRITE/READC/CHAIN/UPDATE, hidden values, SFLNXTCHG scans, response indicators, opaque message keys, live queue SFLINZ/help/paging and job CCSID byte validation use the shared display runtime. Receiver preparation precedes READC acknowledgment. RETURN retains rows and open file/parent leases; subsequent calls rebind the terminal; CLOSE/LR/*NEW/reclaim/job end release allocations. Source/metadata/layout/authority checks and cancellation remain live. Broader native D/C specs, byte-backed arrays/DS/ILE ABI, numeric statuses, printer/messaging callbacks, commitment control, display overrides/ODPs and asynchronous/multiple-device contracts remain open; C09/C11 checklist items stay unchecked. Final RPG WORKSTN verification (2026-10-17): all 1949 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 54 workstation cases and three additional socket variants. The obsolete DCL-S rejection case is removed because the supported scalar declaration now compiles. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled RPG forms/subfiles/message help and suspended-job transfers, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Tests cover private QTEMP layouts, live authority, saved metadata, retained allocations and failed READC retry. Decimal literals, blank-padded comparisons and literal *BLANK input are corrected. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; the broader C09/C11 contracts remain unfinished. |

CL display exchange checkpoint (2026-10-17): DCLF persists selected DSPF records and typed field/indicator layouts; SNDF/RCVF/SNDRCVF drive live local/socket terminals with nested-call resumption, CA/CF semantics, two CCSIDs, packed character buffers and atomic normalized input. Verified built-in System Request group controls and job details preserve paused editors; intentional original-group completion leaves survivors active. Compiled message subfiles retain paging and F1 help after message/private MSGF removal. Thirty new cases cover these contracts, including two socket jobs, cancellation and startup failures. RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT/RRN, asynchronous/multiple-device receives, display overrides/shared ODPs, richer native layout/error fixtures and wider C07/C09/C11 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. [tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/RpgBranchTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/RpgBranchTests.cs); [tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/RpgTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/RpgTests.cs); [tests/Ipc.WorkFixture/Program.cs](../tests/Ipc.WorkFixture/Program.cs) | 998 tests and warning-free Release build pass; real PTY covers named/program inquiries, sender-copy binary keys, replies, message detail and confirmed removal. Schema 20 preserves routing/state/high-water keys; job ownership, inactive-frame cleanup, atomic failures and quotas are covered. Runtime failures reach MONMSG/RCVMSG and propagate once per caller; broader command/runtime, ILE exception and delivery requirements remain open. Byte-function checkpoint: 1023 tests, warning-free build and full PTY pass; hex constants, byte substrings, signed binary fields and *LDA reads/writes are covered. Command-argument checkpoint: 1033 tests and full terminal acceptance pass for qualified/list variables, typed CMD quoting and atomic invalid-input rejection. CALL/cleanup checkpoint: 1081 tests, warning-free build and full PTY pass; direct/compiled native constants, private expression temporaries, aggregate bounds and failure-resilient frame cleanup are covered. Sender checkpoint: 1101 tests, warning-free build and full PTY pass; durable original sender identity/time, independent SHORT/LONG layouts and reply correlation keys are covered. QCMDEXC checkpoint: 1128 tests, expanded real HTTP acceptance, warning-free Release build and full display PTY pass. Direct/compiled CL and RPG calls share command dispatch with byte/packed bounds, same-job state, authority/signature checks, MONMSG and cancellation. Prompting and broader API/debugger contracts remain open. Message-description checkpoint: 1166 tests, warning-free Release build and full display PTY pass. Versioned metadata, native substitution fixtures and atomic CL RTVMSG are covered; predefined queue delivery remains open. Predefined-message delivery snapshots text/help, raw replacement data, severity and inquiry defaults; MONMSG compares replacement prefixes. Tests cover schema 23 preservation, restart, file edits/deletion and authority, CCHAR conversion, output atomicity and metadata quotas. Broader C07/C11 work remains open. Delivery checkpoint: all 1187 tests, warning-free Release build and full display PTY pass, including nested predefined escape/raw MONMSG/RCVMSG acceptance. Inquiry reply rules now snapshot TYPE/LEN/VALUES/SPCVAL/RANGE/REL; 52 additional tests cover compact/quoted special lists, typed/encoded comparisons, defaults, atomic rejection, CL MONMSG, authority, legacy descriptions and restart after file deletion. Real display PTY accepts an operator reply only after validation and confirms RTNTYPE 22. Final reply checkpoint: all 1239 tests pass, no skipped tests, warning-free Release build, full display PTY and generated inventory checks pass. Wider C07/C11 requirements remain open. Data-queue checkpoint: 46 new service/CL/process cases and full display PTY pass. Schema 24 adds durable FIFO/LIFO/keyed entries, exact binary selectors, bounded storage, atomic receive/output failures, waits/cancellation, sender snapshots, authority/revocation/AUTLs/locks and generic lifecycle. Independent processes prove competing delivery; migration, restart and killed waiters are covered. Native data-queue ABI and the wider C07/C11 work remain open. Final data-queue verification: all 1285 tests, warning-free Release build, full display PTY and generated inventory checks pass. An existing SQLite cancellation fixture startup timeout was corrected with a dedicated worker/start signal and unconditional cancellation cleanup. CL display exchange checkpoint (2026-10-17): DCLF persists selected DSPF records and typed field/indicator layouts; SNDF/RCVF/SNDRCVF drive live local/socket terminals with nested-call resumption, CA/CF semantics, two CCSIDs, packed character buffers and atomic normalized input. Display identity/layout/authority and captured adopted authority remain checked while suspended; exchange capabilities expire on return. Initial programs can wait before menu/signoff. Disconnect/ENDJOB joins the worker before frame/QTEMP cleanup. Verified built-in System Request group controls and job details preserve paused editors; intentional original-group completion leaves survivors active. Compiled message subfiles retain paging and F1 help after message/private MSGF removal. Thirty new cases cover these contracts, including two socket jobs, cancellation and startup failures. RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT/RRN, asynchronous/multiple-device receives, display overrides/shared ODPs, richer native layout/error fixtures and wider C07/C09/C11 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Final CL display verification (2026-10-17): all 1893 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 30 new CL display/socket cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with compiled SNDF/RCVF/SNDRCVF fields/indicators/CA/message-subfile help and suspended-job transfers, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT and the documented wider CL/device contracts remain unfinished. Verification history: the first full run passed 1892 cases and timed out once in the existing two-session sign-on test. That case passed an isolated run and five additional repetitions while the full rerun was active; the complete rerun passed all 1893. No timeout or transport behavior was changed for this unreproduced failure. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

CL display exchange checkpoint (2026-10-17): DCLF persists selected DSPF records and typed field/indicator layouts; SNDF/RCVF/SNDRCVF drive live local/socket terminals with nested-call resumption, CA/CF semantics, two CCSIDs, packed character buffers and atomic normalized input. Display identity/layout/authority and captured adopted authority remain checked while suspended; exchange capabilities expire on return. Initial programs can wait before menu/signoff. Disconnect/ENDJOB joins the worker before frame/QTEMP cleanup. Verified built-in System Request group controls and job details preserve paused editors; intentional original-group completion leaves survivors active. Compiled message subfiles retain paging and F1 help after message/private MSGF removal. Thirty new cases cover these contracts, including two socket jobs, cancellation and startup failures. RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT/RRN, asynchronous/multiple-device receives, display overrides/shared ODPs, richer native layout/error fixtures and wider C07/C09/C11 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Final CL display verification (2026-10-17): all 1893 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 30 new CL display/socket cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with compiled SNDF/RCVF/SNDRCVF fields/indicators/CA/message-subfile help and suspended-job transfers, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT and the documented wider CL/device contracts remain unfinished. Verification history: the first full run passed 1892 cases and timed out once in the existing two-session sign-on test. That case passed an isolated run and five additional repetitions while the full rerun was active; the complete rerun passed all 1893. No timeout or transport behavior was changed for this unreproduced failure. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

[tests/Ipc.WorkFixture/Program.cs](../tests/Ipc.WorkFixture/Program.cs) | Owning code/test files only; full requirement acceptance is still open. Scalar prototype API checkpoint (2026-10-17): IPCAPI prototypes now borrow actual CHAR/packed fields with exact formal/actual counts, lengths/scales and live aliases. Typed immutable constants use independent checked buffers; duplicate/missing metadata and unsupported options reject before service mutation. Private named formal storage avoids caller collisions; ordinary targets retain lazy copied argument/writeback semantics. Nested expression separators, literal colons and doubled apostrophes are supported. Full prototype modes, native storage and general ILE binding remain open. Prototype checkpoint verification: 49 new cases and all 1433 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with prototyped keyed queue calls and generated inventory validation pass. RPG preprocessing checkpoint (2026-10-17): CRTBNDRPG expands COPY/INCLUDE, DEFINE/UNDEFINE, IF/ELSEIF/ELSE/ENDIF and listing directives through a separate bounded RPG grammar. Shared live-authority member/UTF-8 resolution supports QRPGLESRC defaults, job library/current-library lookup and relative streams. Expanded snapshots and hash-bound original locations survive copy/source deletion/revocation; compiler/runtime errors retain include chains and CPF metadata. 54 new regression cases and terminal CRTBNDRPG-to-CALL acceptance pass. Broader RPG/ILE/SQL/debugger contracts remain open. Final preprocessing verification: all 1487 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 54 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY and generated inventory checks pass. RPG syntax diagnostics checkpoint (2026-10-17): unknown operations/specifications/D-spec types reject with original source/include locations before any execution. Fixed layout recognition preserves DSPLY DATA/CALLP PRIVATE free text and normalizes distinct fixed subroutine names. A bounded structural pass rejects crossed/unclosed blocks, duplicate branches/names/labels, invalid routine/mode boundaries and ignored control operands. The generated opcode table separates fixed/free recognition and rejects unreachable fixed mappings. 85 new diagnostic/positive regression cases cover mixed modes, scopes and compile/create/call boundaries. Full declaration, expression/type, indicator and native runtime/ILE contracts remain open. Final syntax verification: all 1572 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 85 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with corrected free calls/display and invalid compilation rejection, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. RPG workstation checkpoint (2026-10-17): native/fixed F specs and free DCL-F/DCL-S declarations bind saved DSPF layouts to local/socket EXFMT and separate READ/WRITE. SFILE RRNs, subfile WRITE/READC/CHAIN/UPDATE, hidden values, SFLNXTCHG scans, response indicators, opaque message keys, live queue SFLINZ/help/paging and job CCSID byte validation use the shared display runtime. Receiver preparation precedes READC acknowledgment. RETURN retains rows and open file/parent leases; subsequent calls rebind the terminal; CLOSE/LR/*NEW/reclaim/job end release allocations. Source/metadata/layout/authority checks and cancellation remain live. Broader native D/C specs, byte-backed arrays/DS/ILE ABI, numeric statuses, printer/messaging callbacks, commitment control, display overrides/ODPs and asynchronous/multiple-device contracts remain open; C09/C11 checklist items stay unchecked. Final RPG WORKSTN verification (2026-10-17): all 1949 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 54 workstation cases and three additional socket variants. The obsolete DCL-S rejection case is removed because the supported scalar declaration now compiles. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled RPG forms/subfiles/message help and suspended-job transfers, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Tests cover private QTEMP layouts, live authority, saved metadata, retained allocations and failed READC retry. Decimal literals, blank-padded comparisons and literal *BLANK input are corrected. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; the broader C09/C11 contracts remain unfinished. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |

Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. [tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoApiIntegrationTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoApiIntegrationTests.cs); [tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs](../tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs) | Owning code/test files only; full requirement acceptance is still open. RPG scalar API checkpoint (2026-10-17): direct PLIST and unprototyped inline calls borrow fixed CHAR/packed fields with live alias identity, expired call-scoped cells/factories, per-call job CCSID, count/byte bounds and receiver normalization before dequeue. Ordinary targets retain semantic writeback. Raw API *BLANK bytes remain literal and outer character buffers detach safely into retained RPG text. Prototype receivers and native arrays/DS/binary UTF-8 storage remain open. RPG checkpoint verification: 42 new cases and all 1384 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with direct RPG queue calls and generated inventory checks pass. The existing interpreter cancellation fixtures now use dedicated workers/start signals and unconditional cancellation/await; RPG cancellation is verified against an actual ON-ERROR handler. Scalar prototype API checkpoint (2026-10-17): IPCAPI prototypes now borrow actual CHAR/packed fields with exact formal/actual counts, lengths/scales and live aliases. Typed immutable constants use independent checked buffers; duplicate/missing metadata and unsupported options reject before service mutation. Private named formal storage avoids caller collisions; ordinary targets retain lazy copied argument/writeback semantics. Nested expression separators, literal colons and doubled apostrophes are supported. Full prototype modes, native storage and general ILE binding remain open. Prototype checkpoint verification: 49 new cases and all 1433 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with prototyped keyed queue calls and generated inventory validation pass. RPG preprocessing checkpoint (2026-10-17): CRTBNDRPG expands COPY/INCLUDE, DEFINE/UNDEFINE, IF/ELSEIF/ELSE/ENDIF and listing directives through a separate bounded RPG grammar. Shared live-authority member/UTF-8 resolution supports QRPGLESRC defaults, job library/current-library lookup and relative streams. Expanded snapshots and hash-bound original locations survive copy/source deletion/revocation; compiler/runtime errors retain include chains and CPF metadata. 54 new regression cases and terminal CRTBNDRPG-to-CALL acceptance pass. Broader RPG/ILE/SQL/debugger contracts remain open. Final preprocessing verification: all 1487 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 54 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY and generated inventory checks pass. RPG syntax diagnostics checkpoint (2026-10-17): unknown operations/specifications/D-spec types reject with original source/include locations before any execution. Fixed layout recognition preserves DSPLY DATA/CALLP PRIVATE free text and normalizes distinct fixed subroutine names. A bounded structural pass rejects crossed/unclosed blocks, duplicate branches/names/labels, invalid routine/mode boundaries and ignored control operands. The generated opcode table separates fixed/free recognition and rejects unreachable fixed mappings. 85 new diagnostic/positive regression cases cover mixed modes, scopes and compile/create/call boundaries. Full declaration, expression/type, indicator and native runtime/ILE contracts remain open. Final syntax verification: all 1572 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 85 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with corrected free calls/display and invalid compilation rejection, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. RPG workstation checkpoint (2026-10-17): native/fixed F specs and free DCL-F/DCL-S declarations bind saved DSPF layouts to local/socket EXFMT and separate READ/WRITE. SFILE RRNs, subfile WRITE/READC/CHAIN/UPDATE, hidden values, SFLNXTCHG scans, response indicators, opaque message keys, live queue SFLINZ/help/paging and job CCSID byte validation use the shared display runtime. Receiver preparation precedes READC acknowledgment. RETURN retains rows and open file/parent leases; subsequent calls rebind the terminal; CLOSE/LR/*NEW/reclaim/job end release allocations. Source/metadata/layout/authority checks and cancellation remain live. Broader native D/C specs, byte-backed arrays/DS/ILE ABI, numeric statuses, printer/messaging callbacks, commitment control, display overrides/ODPs and asynchronous/multiple-device contracts remain open; C09/C11 checklist items stay unchecked. Final RPG WORKSTN verification (2026-10-17): all 1949 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 54 workstation cases and three additional socket variants. The obsolete DCL-S rejection case is removed because the supported scalar declaration now compiles. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled RPG forms/subfiles/message help and suspended-job transfers, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Tests cover private QTEMP layouts, live authority, saved metadata, retained allocations and failed READC retry. Decimal literals, blank-padded comparisons and literal *BLANK input are corrected. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; the broader C09/C11 contracts remain unfinished. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Raw EVAL checkpoint (2026-10-17): instructions without result indicators skip numeric result comparison. Arbitrary raw ID bytes now survive %SUBST/EVAL and direct native job API calls at CCSIDs 37/1208; broader RPG contracts remain open. Writable scalar %SUBST checkpoint (2026-10-17): EVAL replaces checked raw/semantic character spans, preserves untouched bytes, pads/truncates selected ranges, retains literal *BLANK and rejects invalid ranges before mutation. Native crypto CL/direct RPG descriptor construction runs at CCSIDs 37/1208. Broader C09 storage/ILE contracts remain open. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

[tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs](../tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs) | Owning code/test files only; full requirement acceptance is still open. RPG scalar API checkpoint (2026-10-17): direct PLIST and unprototyped inline calls borrow fixed CHAR/packed fields with live alias identity, expired call-scoped cells/factories, per-call job CCSID, count/byte bounds and receiver normalization before dequeue. Ordinary targets retain semantic writeback. Raw API *BLANK bytes remain literal and outer character buffers detach safely into retained RPG text. Prototype receivers and native arrays/DS/binary UTF-8 storage remain open. RPG checkpoint verification: 42 new cases and all 1384 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with direct RPG queue calls and generated inventory checks pass. The existing interpreter cancellation fixtures now use dedicated workers/start signals and unconditional cancellation/await; RPG cancellation is verified against an actual ON-ERROR handler. Scalar prototype API checkpoint (2026-10-17): IPCAPI prototypes now borrow actual CHAR/packed fields with exact formal/actual counts, lengths/scales and live aliases. Typed immutable constants use independent checked buffers; duplicate/missing metadata and unsupported options reject before service mutation. Private named formal storage avoids caller collisions; ordinary targets retain lazy copied argument/writeback semantics. Nested expression separators, literal colons and doubled apostrophes are supported. Full prototype modes, native storage and general ILE binding remain open. Prototype checkpoint verification: 49 new cases and all 1433 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with prototyped keyed queue calls and generated inventory validation pass. RPG preprocessing checkpoint (2026-10-17): CRTBNDRPG expands COPY/INCLUDE, DEFINE/UNDEFINE, IF/ELSEIF/ELSE/ENDIF and listing directives through a separate bounded RPG grammar. Shared live-authority member/UTF-8 resolution supports QRPGLESRC defaults, job library/current-library lookup and relative streams. Expanded snapshots and hash-bound original locations survive copy/source deletion/revocation; compiler/runtime errors retain include chains and CPF metadata. 54 new regression cases and terminal CRTBNDRPG-to-CALL acceptance pass. Broader RPG/ILE/SQL/debugger contracts remain open. Final preprocessing verification: all 1487 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 54 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY and generated inventory checks pass. RPG syntax diagnostics checkpoint (2026-10-17): unknown operations/specifications/D-spec types reject with original source/include locations before any execution. Fixed layout recognition preserves DSPLY DATA/CALLP PRIVATE free text and normalizes distinct fixed subroutine names. A bounded structural pass rejects crossed/unclosed blocks, duplicate branches/names/labels, invalid routine/mode boundaries and ignored control operands. The generated opcode table separates fixed/free recognition and rejects unreachable fixed mappings. 85 new diagnostic/positive regression cases cover mixed modes, scopes and compile/create/call boundaries. Full declaration, expression/type, indicator and native runtime/ILE contracts remain open. Final syntax verification: all 1572 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 85 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with corrected free calls/display and invalid compilation rejection, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. RPG workstation checkpoint (2026-10-17): native/fixed F specs and free DCL-F/DCL-S declarations bind saved DSPF layouts to local/socket EXFMT and separate READ/WRITE. SFILE RRNs, subfile WRITE/READC/CHAIN/UPDATE, hidden values, SFLNXTCHG scans, response indicators, opaque message keys, live queue SFLINZ/help/paging and job CCSID byte validation use the shared display runtime. Receiver preparation precedes READC acknowledgment. RETURN retains rows and open file/parent leases; subsequent calls rebind the terminal; CLOSE/LR/*NEW/reclaim/job end release allocations. Source/metadata/layout/authority checks and cancellation remain live. Broader native D/C specs, byte-backed arrays/DS/ILE ABI, numeric statuses, printer/messaging callbacks, commitment control, display overrides/ODPs and asynchronous/multiple-device contracts remain open; C09/C11 checklist items stay unchecked. Final RPG WORKSTN verification (2026-10-17): all 1949 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 54 workstation cases and three additional socket variants. The obsolete DCL-S rejection case is removed because the supported scalar declaration now compiles. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled RPG forms/subfiles/message help and suspended-job transfers, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Tests cover private QTEMP layouts, live authority, saved metadata, retained allocations and failed READC retry. Decimal literals, blank-padded comparisons and literal *BLANK input are corrected. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; the broader C09/C11 contracts remain unfinished. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Raw EVAL checkpoint (2026-10-17): instructions without result indicators skip numeric result comparison. Arbitrary raw ID bytes now survive %SUBST/EVAL and direct native job API calls at CCSIDs 37/1208; broader RPG contracts remain open. Writable scalar %SUBST checkpoint (2026-10-17): EVAL replaces checked raw/semantic character spans, preserves untouched bytes, pads/truncates selected ranges, retains literal *BLANK and rejects invalid ranges before mutation. Native crypto CL/direct RPG descriptor construction runs at CCSIDs 37/1208. Broader C09 storage/ILE contracts remain open. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

Source/metadata/layout/authority checks and cancellation remain live. Tests cover private QTEMP layouts, live authority, saved metadata, retained allocations and failed READC retry. Decimal literals, blank-padded comparisons and literal *BLANK input are corrected. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Native queue API checkpoint (2026-10-17): Ipc.Api implements local STD QSNDDTAQ/QRCVDTAQ/QCLRDTAQ with checked packed fields, complete optional groups, atomic writable receivers, truncation/peek/timeout, sender headers and big-endian error receivers. 57 new cases pass, covering independent byte vectors, CCSIDs 37/1208, maximum lengths, unchanged outputs/tails, MONMSG, RPG through a typed CL wrapper, cancellation, live authority and signing trust. Direct RPG receive borrowing, exact native error distinctions, DDM, journals and broader APIs remain open. Final verification: all 1342 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY including native queue APIs and generated inventory validation pass. Startup acceptance now verifies the updated seed count and all four IPCAPI manifests. RPG scalar API checkpoint (2026-10-17): direct PLIST and unprototyped inline calls borrow fixed CHAR/packed fields with live alias identity, expired call-scoped cells/factories, per-call job CCSID, count/byte bounds and receiver normalization before dequeue. Ordinary targets retain semantic writeback. Raw API *BLANK bytes remain literal and outer character buffers detach safely into retained RPG text. Prototype receivers and native arrays/DS/binary UTF-8 storage remain open. RPG checkpoint verification: 42 new cases and all 1384 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with direct RPG queue calls and generated inventory checks pass. The existing interpreter cancellation fixtures now use dedicated workers/start signals and unconditional cancellation/await; RPG cancellation is verified against an actual ON-ERROR handler. Scalar prototype API checkpoint (2026-10-17): IPCAPI prototypes now borrow actual CHAR/packed fields with exact formal/actual counts, lengths/scales and live aliases. Typed immutable constants use independent checked buffers; duplicate/missing metadata and unsupported options reject before service mutation. Private named formal storage avoids caller collisions; ordinary targets retain lazy copied argument/writeback semantics. Nested expression separators, literal colons and doubled apostrophes are supported. Full prototype modes, native storage and general ILE binding remain open. Prototype checkpoint verification: 49 new cases and all 1433 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with prototyped keyed queue calls and generated inventory validation pass. QTEMP namespace checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-26 runtime-owned private libraries initialize atomically with jobs and for existing live jobs after upgrade. Catalog, user spaces, typed data areas, STD queues, inline CL program objects, authority and allocations resolve the executing job; even privileged foreign-job backing-name access is denied. Native return names stay QTEMP. Submission/groups receive separate namespaces. Completion/cancellation and exclusive-owner crash recovery clean payloads and bindings atomically, preserving unclaimed queued jobs. Private files and other specialized stores, generic user-space deletion, MI/list behavior and full lock/error/domain relocation fidelity remain open; broad C11/C14 items remain unchecked. Final QTEMP verification (2026-10-17): all 1689 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 25 new namespace/storage/ABI/security/process/server cases and eight additional migration-version cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with QTEMP user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Job-history deletion acceptance now completes jobs before purging their rows, preserving namespace cleanup and non-reused job numbers. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. |

Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including the native binary user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. QTEMP namespace checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-26 runtime-owned private libraries initialize atomically with jobs and for existing live jobs after upgrade. Catalog, user spaces, typed data areas, STD queues, inline CL program objects, authority and allocations resolve the executing job; even privileged foreign-job backing-name access is denied. Native return names stay QTEMP. Submission/groups receive separate namespaces. Completion/cancellation and exclusive-owner crash recovery clean payloads and bindings atomically, preserving unclaimed queued jobs. Private files and other specialized stores, generic user-space deletion, MI/list behavior and full lock/error/domain relocation fidelity remain open; broad C11/C14 items remain unchecked. Final QTEMP verification (2026-10-17): all 1689 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 25 new namespace/storage/ABI/security/process/server cases and eight additional migration-version cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with QTEMP user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Job-history deletion acceptance now completes jobs before purging their rows, preserving namespace cleanup and non-reused job numbers. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. User-space lifecycle checkpoint (2026-10-17): generic DLTUSRSPC/QUSDLTUS prefix selection and *LIBL/*CURLIB/*USRLIBL/*ALL/*ALLUSR selectors, partial commit with CPF2117/CPF2125, dependency savepoints, cancellation/database-fault rollback and private namespace isolation are implemented. Native EXCLRD writes and all 25 lock compatibility combinations, CL allocation/inspection/release and record intent are tested. Live QALWUSRDMN mixed lists require ALLOBJ plus SECADM, preserve explicit SYSTEM in QTEMP and enforce policy inside copy/move/library-rename transactions. Same-library renames preserve existing domains; disallowed user-domain creation returns CPF3C49. Rich exception data, MI/list producers and other QTEMP domain stores remain broader C11/C14 work. Final lifecycle verification (2026-10-17): all 1763 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 53 additional user-space/deletion/lock/domain cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with generic QTEMP deletion and EXCLRD allocations, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. WP12-04 byte-store acceptance is complete under its documented CRTUSRSPC/QUSCRTUS/QUSRUSAT subset; wider C11/C14 checklist items remain open (35 complete / 71 open). Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. |
