user-space and journal acceptance remain open. C11 private job-area storage acceptance (2026-10-17): automatic CHAR LDA creation and atomic submission copies, shared GDA/group lifetime, and the previously documented C04 raw routing-PIP extension are audited complete. 27 additional cases cover seven CCSIDs, exact bounds/detached reads, queued restart, failed-claim rollback, pending-payload clearing, job-identity denial, process concurrency and cancellation/cleanup. Missing GDA/PDA return CPF1046/CPF1072 through MONMSG. Full terminal group/secondary transfers preserve shared GDA, independent LDAs/groups and edited panels. Native prestart protocols and broader work/API/typed-data-area requirements remain open. Final job-area verification: all 1599 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 27 new cases; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with group data-area acceptance, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. User-space lifecycle checkpoint (2026-10-17): generic DLTUSRSPC/QUSDLTUS prefix selection and *LIBL/*CURLIB/*USRLIBL/*ALL/*ALLUSR selectors, partial commit with CPF2117/CPF2125, dependency savepoints, cancellation/database-fault rollback and private namespace isolation are implemented. Native EXCLRD writes and all 25 lock compatibility combinations, CL allocation/inspection/release and record intent are tested. Live QALWUSRDMN mixed lists require ALLOBJ plus SECADM, preserve explicit SYSTEM in QTEMP and enforce policy inside copy/move/library-rename transactions. Same-library renames preserve existing domains; disallowed user-domain creation returns CPF3C49. Rich exception data, MI/list producers and other QTEMP domain stores remain broader C11/C14 work. Final lifecycle verification (2026-10-17): all 1763 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 53 additional user-space/deletion/lock/domain cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with generic QTEMP deletion and EXCLRD allocations, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. WP12-04 byte-store acceptance is complete under its documented CRTUSRSPC/QUSCRTUS/QUSRUSAT subset; wider C11/C14 checklist items remain open (35 complete / 71 open). Data-area API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRDTAA is a versioned IPCAPI program over the existing typed named/job-area stores. Checked big-endian headers, packed DEC values through 24 digits, logical encoding, truncated receivers, error buffers, strict CCSID conversion, first-match library lookup, QTEMP isolation and special-area ownership are implemented. Forty-five new cases cover independent byte vectors, CL and direct borrowed RPG calls, live authority/signing revocation, allocations, ranges, cancellation and the documented routing-PIP extension. Native DDM retrieval, independent padding/CCSID-position fixtures and richer exception data remain open; the broad C11/C14 checklist stays unchecked. Final QWCRDTAA verification (2026-10-17): all 1808 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 45 new data-area API cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including native decimal/header/LDA layouts and direct borrowed RPG retrieval, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; native DDM retrieval, padding/CCSID-position acceptance and wider C11/C14 work remain unfinished. Private messaging checkpoint (2026-10-17): MSGQ and MSGF operations resolve QTEMP before SQL access. Private inquiry reply routes grant exact system delivery to a live originating job without granting foreign read/send/library access; reply locks and sender metadata belong to the replier. Atomic batch sends, quota/default/rule failures, copies/exports, queue replacement waits, job cleanup and exclusive-server recovery retain their invariants. Version-3 predefined snapshots preserve private source identity internally while exposing logical metadata; legacy snapshots are sanitized in the read snapshot and retired atomically at cleanup. Separate producer/consumer/reply processes, legacy compatibility, two CCSIDs, private CL/DSPMSG and rollback tests cover these changes. The broad C07/C11/C14 checklist remains open for delivery attributes, break/notify, subfiles and wider APIs. Final private messaging verification (2026-10-17): all 1839 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 31 new private message cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with private MSGQ/MSGF/predefined/CL/display/remove lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; delivery attributes, break/notify, message-subfile bindings and wider APIs remain unfinished. Message-subfile binding checkpoint (2026-10-17): live job/frame queue sources resolve output callers, external queues and nearest recursive programs without foreign-job access. Row writes capture text/help; SFLINZ reads a single bounded queue snapshot and grows the subfile beyond SFLSIZ. Display reads leave seen, exception, inquiry and reply state unchanged. Paging/help survive queue entry and private MSGF removal; failed initialization/output restores rows, capacity, page and editor. Twenty-four new tests and a live terminal fixture cover opaque keys, two CCSIDs, 80/132-column output, more than 1000 messages, authority and DDS diagnostics. CL SNDRCVF, RPG WORKSTN/EXFMT, record/RRN bindings, terminal suspension and ILE 276-byte queue names remain open; broad C07/C09/C11 checklist items stay unchecked. Final message-subfile verification (2026-10-17): all 1863 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 24 new message-subfile cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including live SFLINZ/paging/saved second-level help after source removal, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; CL/RPG terminal exchange and broader C07/C09/C11 work remain unfinished. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. |

[tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoContextTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoContextTests.cs); [tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs](../tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs) | Native queue API checkpoint (2026-10-17): Ipc.Api implements local STD QSNDDTAQ/QRCVDTAQ/QCLRDTAQ with checked packed fields, complete optional groups, atomic writable receivers, truncation/peek/timeout, sender headers and big-endian error receivers. 57 new cases pass, covering independent byte vectors, CCSIDs 37/1208, maximum lengths, unchanged outputs/tails, MONMSG, RPG through a typed CL wrapper, cancellation, live authority and signing trust. Direct RPG receive borrowing, exact native error distinctions, DDM, journals and broader APIs remain open. Final verification: all 1342 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY including native queue APIs and generated inventory validation pass. Startup acceptance now verifies the updated seed count and all four IPCAPI manifests. RPG scalar API checkpoint (2026-10-17): direct PLIST and unprototyped inline calls borrow fixed CHAR/packed fields with live alias identity, expired call-scoped cells/factories, per-call job CCSID, count/byte bounds and receiver normalization before dequeue. Ordinary targets retain semantic writeback. Raw API *BLANK bytes remain literal and outer character buffers detach safely into retained RPG text. Prototype receivers and native arrays/DS/binary UTF-8 storage remain open. RPG checkpoint verification: 42 new cases and all 1384 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with direct RPG queue calls and generated inventory checks pass. The existing interpreter cancellation fixtures now use dedicated workers/start signals and unconditional cancellation/await; RPG cancellation is verified against an actual ON-ERROR handler. Scalar prototype API checkpoint (2026-10-17): IPCAPI prototypes now borrow actual CHAR/packed fields with exact formal/actual counts, lengths/scales and live aliases. Typed immutable constants use independent checked buffers; duplicate/missing metadata and unsupported options reject before service mutation. Private named formal storage avoids caller collisions; ordinary targets retain lazy copied argument/writeback semantics. Nested expression separators, literal colons and doubled apostrophes are supported. Full prototype modes, native storage and general ILE binding remain open. Prototype checkpoint verification: 49 new cases and all 1433 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with prototyped keyed queue calls and generated inventory validation pass. User-space checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-25 opaque byte storage, native maximum sizes, one-based read/write and atomic resize, independent copy/move/delete/library rename, live authority/locks/cancellation, replacement with authority-preserving QRPLOBJ archival/domain-policy deletion and exclusive-server startup recovery. Six QSYS IPCAPI adapters implement native binary parameters, SPCA0100 attributes, aligned keyed attribute records, optional groups and checked writable/error receivers. CRTUSRSPC extension and exact-name DLTUSRSPC are executable. QTEMP job-private object storage, generic deletion names, MI/list APIs, full native lock/error/domain relocation behavior remain open; C11/C14 stay unchecked. Final user-space verification (2026-10-17): all 1656 tests pass with zero failures/skips, including 57 new storage/ABI/restart cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including the native binary user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. QTEMP namespace checkpoint (2026-10-17): schema-26 runtime-owned private libraries initialize atomically with jobs and for existing live jobs after upgrade. Catalog, user spaces, typed data areas, STD queues, inline CL program objects, authority and allocations resolve the executing job; even privileged foreign-job backing-name access is denied. Native return names stay QTEMP. Submission/groups receive separate namespaces. Completion/cancellation and exclusive-owner crash recovery clean payloads and bindings atomically, preserving unclaimed queued jobs. Private files and other specialized stores, generic user-space deletion, MI/list behavior and full lock/error/domain relocation fidelity remain open; broad C11/C14 items remain unchecked. Final QTEMP verification (2026-10-17): all 1689 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 25 new namespace/storage/ABI/security/process/server cases and eight additional migration-version cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY with QTEMP user-space lifecycle, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Job-history deletion acceptance now completes jobs before purging their rows, preserving namespace cleanup and non-reused job numbers. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native date API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCCVTDT now accepts complete 5/10/11-argument groups and native short/long calendar/Julian date-time layouts, CURRENT/SYSVAL/DTS and DOS formats. The shared timestamp codec preserves standard 52-bit microsecond/12-bit uniqueness semantics; the API applies its eight-microsecond conversion quantum and native millisecond rounding/ranges/wrap rules. Character conversion preserves unvalidated time bytes and short century copy behavior. UTC/actual host-zone conversion validates DST gaps, supports both repeated-time segments and rounds on the UTC instant across transitions. UTC/out-of-range information receivers use zero binary/blank character fields and preserve tails. Compiled CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208, live program authority/signing/manifests, output preparation/cancellation and real terminal flows have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects and in-range host-zone metadata are explicitly unsupported pending shared services. Wider C14 API/crypto requirements remain open: 35 complete / 71 open. See docs/date-time-apis.md for exact layouts, selectors and errors. Final native date API verification (2026-10-17): all 2240 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 new timestamp/layout/range/copy/DST/DOS/control/error/cancellation and CL/RPG integration cases. All 74 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG microsecond timestamp round-trips and WRKAPILS details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published MI vectors verify the shared full-precision codec; QWCCVTDT conversion applies an eight-microsecond quantum inferred from IBM precision notes/endpoints. Native copy rules preserve raw time/century bytes where validation is not required. Rounding across DST and New Year, invalid local times, both repeated-time segments and atomic output preparation are verified. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects, in-range host-zone metadata and wider API/crypto families remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native cryptography checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS QC3 adapters now provide hash/HMAC, AES encrypt/decrypt and opaque job-owned key contexts through shared .NET services. Five hashes, three AES key sizes, ECB/CBC/CTR, native padding, checked DATA0100/ALGD0500/ALGD0200/KEYD0100/0200 descriptors and atomic output/ length/error preparation have published known-answer acceptance. Schema 29 stores keys under authenticated AES-GCM envelopes, preserves existing catalog data/IDs, checks current job/profile ownership, limits contexts per job, supports wrapping inventory/rotation and deletes contexts with completion/crash recovery. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and real PTY run all six programs; independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST now constructs raw descriptors with checked bounds, exact byte tails and literal-text preservation. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters. Streaming algorithm contexts, persistent native keystores, additional cipher/format families and ILE binding retain their broader open requirements. See docs/cryptography-apis.md. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

User-space lifecycle checkpoint (2026-10-17): generic DLTUSRSPC/QUSDLTUS prefix selection and *LIBL/*CURLIB/*USRLIBL/*ALL/*ALLUSR selectors, partial commit with CPF2117/CPF2125, dependency savepoints, cancellation/database-fault rollback and private namespace isolation are implemented. Native EXCLRD writes and all 25 lock compatibility combinations, CL allocation/inspection/release and record intent are tested. Live QALWUSRDMN mixed lists require ALLOBJ plus SECADM, preserve explicit SYSTEM in QTEMP and enforce policy inside copy/move/library-rename transactions. Same-library renames preserve existing domains; disallowed user-domain creation returns CPF3C49. Rich exception data, MI/list producers and other QTEMP domain stores remain broader C11/C14 work. Final lifecycle verification (2026-10-17): all 1763 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 53 additional user-space/deletion/lock/domain cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with generic QTEMP deletion and EXCLRD allocations, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. WP12-04 byte-store acceptance is complete under its documented CRTUSRSPC/QUSCRTUS/QUSRUSAT subset; wider C11/C14 checklist items remain open (35 complete / 71 open). Data-area API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRDTAA is a versioned IPCAPI program over the existing typed named/job-area stores. Checked big-endian headers, packed DEC values through 24 digits, logical encoding, truncated receivers, error buffers, strict CCSID conversion, first-match library lookup, QTEMP isolation and special-area ownership are implemented. Forty-five new cases cover independent byte vectors, CL and direct borrowed RPG calls, live authority/signing revocation, allocations, ranges, cancellation and the documented routing-PIP extension. Native DDM retrieval, independent padding/CCSID-position fixtures and richer exception data remain open; the broad C11/C14 checklist stays unchecked. Final QWCRDTAA verification (2026-10-17): all 1808 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 45 new data-area API cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including native decimal/header/LDA layouts and direct borrowed RPG retrieval, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; native DDM retrieval, padding/CCSID-position acceptance and wider C11/C14 work remain unfinished. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native date API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCCVTDT now accepts complete 5/10/11-argument groups and native short/long calendar/Julian date-time layouts, CURRENT/SYSVAL/DTS and DOS formats. The shared timestamp codec preserves standard 52-bit microsecond/12-bit uniqueness semantics; the API applies its eight-microsecond conversion quantum and native millisecond rounding/ranges/wrap rules. Character conversion preserves unvalidated time bytes and short century copy behavior. UTC/actual host-zone conversion validates DST gaps, supports both repeated-time segments and rounds on the UTC instant across transitions. UTC/out-of-range information receivers use zero binary/blank character fields and preserve tails. Compiled CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208, live program authority/signing/manifests, output preparation/cancellation and real terminal flows have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects and in-range host-zone metadata are explicitly unsupported pending shared services. Wider C14 API/crypto requirements remain open: 35 complete / 71 open. See docs/date-time-apis.md for exact layouts, selectors and errors. Final native date API verification (2026-10-17): all 2240 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 new timestamp/layout/range/copy/DST/DOS/control/error/cancellation and CL/RPG integration cases. All 74 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG microsecond timestamp round-trips and WRKAPILS details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published MI vectors verify the shared full-precision codec; QWCCVTDT conversion applies an eight-microsecond quantum inferred from IBM precision notes/endpoints. Native copy rules preserve raw time/century bytes where validation is not required. Rounding across DST and New Year, invalid local times, both repeated-time segments and atomic output preparation are verified. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects, in-range host-zone metadata and wider API/crypto families remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native cryptography checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS QC3 adapters now provide hash/HMAC, AES encrypt/decrypt and opaque job-owned key contexts through shared .NET services. Five hashes, three AES key sizes, ECB/CBC/CTR, native padding, checked DATA0100/ALGD0500/ALGD0200/KEYD0100/0200 descriptors and atomic output/ length/error preparation have published known-answer acceptance. Schema 29 stores keys under authenticated AES-GCM envelopes, preserves existing catalog data/IDs, checks current job/profile ownership, limits contexts per job, supports wrapping inventory/rotation and deletes contexts with completion/crash recovery. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and real PTY run all six programs; independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST now constructs raw descriptors with checked bounds, exact byte tails and literal-text preservation. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters. Streaming algorithm contexts, persistent native keystores, additional cipher/format families and ILE binding retain their broader open requirements. See docs/cryptography-apis.md. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

[tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoContextTests.cs](../tests/Ipc.Core.Tests/CryptoContextTests.cs); [tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs](../tests/Shared/CryptoProgramSources.cs) | QCMDEXC checkpoint: 1128 tests, expanded real HTTP acceptance, warning-free Release build and full display PTY pass. Direct/compiled CL and RPG calls share command dispatch with byte/packed bounds, same-job state, authority/signature checks, MONMSG and cancellation. Prompting and broader API/debugger contracts remain open. Native queue API checkpoint (2026-10-17): Ipc.Api implements local STD QSNDDTAQ/QRCVDTAQ/QCLRDTAQ with checked packed fields, complete optional groups, atomic writable receivers, truncation/peek/timeout, sender headers and big-endian error receivers. 57 new cases pass, covering independent byte vectors, CCSIDs 37/1208, maximum lengths, unchanged outputs/tails, MONMSG, RPG through a typed CL wrapper, cancellation, live authority and signing trust. Direct RPG receive borrowing, exact native error distinctions, DDM, journals and broader APIs remain open. Final verification: all 1342 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full display PTY including native queue APIs and generated inventory validation pass. Startup acceptance now verifies the updated seed count and all four IPCAPI manifests. RPG scalar API checkpoint (2026-10-17): direct PLIST and unprototyped inline calls borrow fixed CHAR/packed fields with live alias identity, expired call-scoped cells/factories, per-call job CCSID, count/byte bounds and receiver normalization before dequeue. Ordinary targets retain semantic writeback. Raw API *BLANK bytes remain literal and outer character buffers detach safely into retained RPG text. Prototype receivers and native arrays/DS/binary UTF-8 storage remain open. RPG checkpoint verification: 42 new cases and all 1384 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with direct RPG queue calls and generated inventory checks pass. The existing interpreter cancellation fixtures now use dedicated workers/start signals and unconditional cancellation/await; RPG cancellation is verified against an actual ON-ERROR handler. Scalar prototype API checkpoint (2026-10-17): IPCAPI prototypes now borrow actual CHAR/packed fields with exact formal/actual counts, lengths/scales and live aliases. Typed immutable constants use independent checked buffers; duplicate/missing metadata and unsupported options reject before service mutation. Private named formal storage avoids caller collisions; ordinary targets retain lazy copied argument/writeback semantics. Nested expression separators, literal colons and doubled apostrophes are supported. Full prototype modes, native storage and general ILE binding remain open. Prototype checkpoint verification: 49 new cases and all 1433 tests pass, zero failures/skips; locked restore, zero-warning/error Release build, full terminal PTY with prototyped keyed queue calls and generated inventory validation pass. User-space lifecycle checkpoint (2026-10-17): generic DLTUSRSPC/QUSDLTUS prefix selection and *LIBL/*CURLIB/*USRLIBL/*ALL/*ALLUSR selectors, partial commit with CPF2117/CPF2125, dependency savepoints, cancellation/database-fault rollback and private namespace isolation are implemented. Native EXCLRD writes and all 25 lock compatibility combinations, CL allocation/inspection/release and record intent are tested. Live QALWUSRDMN mixed lists require ALLOBJ plus SECADM, preserve explicit SYSTEM in QTEMP and enforce policy inside copy/move/library-rename transactions. Same-library renames preserve existing domains; disallowed user-domain creation returns CPF3C49. Rich exception data, MI/list producers and other QTEMP domain stores remain broader C11/C14 work. Final lifecycle verification (2026-10-17): all 1763 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 53 additional user-space/deletion/lock/domain cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY with generic QTEMP deletion and EXCLRD allocations, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. WP12-04 byte-store acceptance is complete under its documented CRTUSRSPC/QUSCRTUS/QUSRUSAT subset; wider C11/C14 checklist items remain open (35 complete / 71 open). Data-area API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRDTAA is a versioned IPCAPI program over the existing typed named/job-area stores. Checked big-endian headers, packed DEC values through 24 digits, logical encoding, truncated receivers, error buffers, strict CCSID conversion, first-match library lookup, QTEMP isolation and special-area ownership are implemented. Forty-five new cases cover independent byte vectors, CL and direct borrowed RPG calls, live authority/signing revocation, allocations, ranges, cancellation and the documented routing-PIP extension. Native DDM retrieval, independent padding/CCSID-position fixtures and richer exception data remain open; the broad C11/C14 checklist stays unchecked. Final QWCRDTAA verification (2026-10-17): all 1808 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 45 new data-area API cases. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, full display PTY including native decimal/header/LDA layouts and direct borrowed RPG retrieval, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open; native DDM retrieval, padding/CCSID-position acceptance and wider C11/C14 work remain unfinished. Native message API checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS adapters implement finite QMHSNDPM/QMHRCVPM/QMHSNDM/QMHRCVM/QMHSNDRM/QMHRTVM contracts over shared named/program queues and saved descriptions. RCVM0100/0200 and RTVM0100 use checked binary receivers, opaque keys, conversion statuses, truncation and prepared output before state/removal/reply commits. SAME preserves state; inquiry copy-key replies, atomic multicast/default replies, native OPM frame selection excluding API frames, QTEMP isolation and cancellable allocated waits are covered. Schema 27 preserves old bytes/metadata/key high-water marks and rolls back failed rebuilds. RPG supports raw CHAR CCSID(*HEX)/65535, hex literals/byte substrings and signed INT API references. Direct CL/RPG tests run at CCSIDs 37/1208; terminal acceptance covers native RPG send/receive/retrieve, message-subfile F1 help and registry details. 199 focused cases and expanded PTY pass. Pointer/qualified ILE selectors, richer formats, larger replacement data, rejection exits, native exception/UI dispatch, messaging callbacks and wider C09/C11/C14 behavior remain open. Final native message/registry verification (2026-10-17): all 2036 tests pass, zero failures/skips. Locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native RPG send/receive/retrieve and saved message help, WRKAPILS list/details/back, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Independent vectors cover CYYMMDD century boundaries, native sender/profile offsets, incomplete groups, invalid receivers, FIRST/NEXT retrieval and empty-table deleted-key preservation. Independent socket jobs verify allocated native receive, conflicting receiver, delivery and lease cleanup. WP15-03 is supported; the broad checklist remains 35 complete / 71 open. System-value API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCRSVAL reads 27 backed native values from one committed catalog snapshot and one local clock capture. It checks five parameters, binary sizes/counts, fixed-name arrays, offset-directory/table layouts, four-byte alignment and complete-entry truncation without modifying tails. Native character/binary types, policy aliases and fixed library/domain arrays are explicit. CL/RPG callers, shared/reopened hosts, separate-process atomic snapshots, authority/signing/manifests and socket-job changes have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS now discovers 18 adapters. System-value library defaults are bounded to native 15/25 names, separate from running-job 250. Legacy oversized values fail without truncation. A false RPG IF now enters its ELSE body, with fixed/free/nested regression cases. Protected/lockable values, wider native catalog/formats and broader C09/C14/C18 contracts remain open. Final system-value API verification (2026-10-17): all 2108 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 72 new native-layout, policy, clock, process/socket, CL/RPG and branch cases. All 109 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG QWCRSVAL and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. QWCRSVAL provides 27 backed native values; WRKAPILS discovers 18 adapters. Shared snapshots observe committed updates, dates/times use one live capture, and native 15/25-name defaults preserve oversized legacy data with actionable retrieval errors. False RPG IF/ELSE and nested/fixed/free paths are corrected. Protected/lockable system values, wider API families and broader C09/C14/C18 work remain open; checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native job API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100 basic metadata and protected active-job JOBI0700 libraries; QUSLJOB produces JOBL0100 and JOBL0200 with 17 backed keys. Shared schema-28 opaque IDs and authenticated 48-byte cursors persist across ordinary catalog opens and refresh atomically at exclusive host startup. List publication preserves caller/tail bytes, implicitly grows existing spaces regardless of auto-extension, checks live authority and EXCLRD allocations, and rolls back bytes/size/change state on failure/cancellation. Real maximum-space continuation covers 3000 jobs and 64 repeated keys, tampered, foreign, mismatched and stale handles. Terminal history is not mislabeled OUTQ. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and independent socket QTEMP jobs pass. Raw RPG EVAL now avoids numeric indicator comparison when no result indicators exist. WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. See docs/job-apis.md for layouts, bounds and finite semantics. Wider job formats, unbacked counters, spool integration and remaining API/cryptography families keep C09/C14 open: 35 complete / 71 open. Final native job API verification (2026-10-17): all 2174 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 additional job-layout, opaque-ID, migration, cursor, CL/RPG, socket and raw-EVAL cases. All 154 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with native CL/direct RPG job list/retrieval and registry formats, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Schema-28 backfill and exclusive-host epoch refresh preserve job data; list generation preserves caller/tail bytes, grows implicitly and continues at the real maximum with authenticated handles. QUSRJOBI supports JOBI0100/0700 and QUSLJOB supports JOBL0100/0200 with 17 backed keys; WRKAPILS discovers 20 adapters. Wider API families/formats, performance statistics, spool integration and broader C09/C14 work remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native date API checkpoint (2026-10-17): QSYS/QWCCVTDT now accepts complete 5/10/11-argument groups and native short/long calendar/Julian date-time layouts, CURRENT/SYSVAL/DTS and DOS formats. The shared timestamp codec preserves standard 52-bit microsecond/12-bit uniqueness semantics; the API applies its eight-microsecond conversion quantum and native millisecond rounding/ranges/wrap rules. Character conversion preserves unvalidated time bytes and short century copy behavior. UTC/actual host-zone conversion validates DST gaps, supports both repeated-time segments and rounds on the UTC instant across transitions. UTC/out-of-range information receivers use zero binary/blank character fields and preserve tails. Compiled CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208, live program authority/signing/manifests, output preparation/cancellation and real terminal flows have acceptance coverage. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects and in-range host-zone metadata are explicitly unsupported pending shared services. Wider C14 API/crypto requirements remain open: 35 complete / 71 open. See docs/date-time-apis.md for exact layouts, selectors and errors. Final native date API verification (2026-10-17): all 2240 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 66 new timestamp/layout/range/copy/DST/DOS/control/error/cancellation and CL/RPG integration cases. All 74 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with compiled CL/direct RPG microsecond timestamp round-trips and WRKAPILS details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published MI vectors verify the shared full-precision codec; QWCCVTDT conversion applies an eight-microsecond quantum inferred from IBM precision notes/endpoints. Native copy rules preserve raw time/century bytes where validation is not required. Rounding across DST and New Year, invalid local times, both repeated-time segments and atomic output preparation are verified. WRKAPILS discovers 21 adapters. Job DATFMT/time-zone attributes, named TIMZON objects, in-range host-zone metadata and wider API/crypto families remain open. Checklist stays 35 complete / 71 open. Native cryptography checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS QC3 adapters now provide hash/HMAC, AES encrypt/decrypt and opaque job-owned key contexts through shared .NET services. Five hashes, three AES key sizes, ECB/CBC/CTR, native padding, checked DATA0100/ALGD0500/ALGD0200/KEYD0100/0200 descriptors and atomic output/ length/error preparation have published known-answer acceptance. Schema 29 stores keys under authenticated AES-GCM envelopes, preserves existing catalog data/IDs, checks current job/profile ownership, limits contexts per job, supports wrapping inventory/rotation and deletes contexts with completion/crash recovery. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and real PTY run all six programs; independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST now constructs raw descriptors with checked bounds, exact byte tails and literal-text preservation. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters. Streaming algorithm contexts, persistent native keystores, additional cipher/format families and ILE binding retain their broader open requirements. See docs/cryptography-apis.md. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

IDS/VPN, host adapters and remaining crypto APIs remain in their owning tasks. Native cryptography checkpoint (2026-10-17): six QSYS QC3 adapters now provide hash/HMAC, AES encrypt/decrypt and opaque job-owned key contexts through shared .NET services. Five hashes, three AES key sizes, ECB/CBC/CTR, native padding, checked DATA0100/ALGD0500/ALGD0200/KEYD0100/0200 descriptors and atomic output/ length/error preparation have published known-answer acceptance. Schema 29 stores keys under authenticated AES-GCM envelopes, preserves existing catalog data/IDs, checks current job/profile ownership, limits contexts per job, supports wrapping inventory/rotation and deletes contexts with completion/crash recovery. CL/direct RPG at CCSIDs 37/1208 and real PTY run all six programs; independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST now constructs raw descriptors with checked bounds, exact byte tails and literal-text preservation. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters. Streaming algorithm contexts, persistent native keystores, additional cipher/format families and ILE binding retain their broader open requirements. See docs/cryptography-apis.md. Final C14 cryptography verification (2026-10-17): all 2309 tests pass, zero failures/skips, including 69 new crypto, key-context, migration, native CL/RPG/socket and writable-substring cases. All 176 focused cases, locked restore, zero-warning/error Release solution build, expanded display PTY with all six QC3 adapters and registry details, generated inventory and whitespace checks pass. Published NIST/RFC vectors cover five hashes/HMACs and AES 128/192/256 in ECB/CBC/CTR; native padding, key types/lengths, maximum key size, per-job limits, atomic receivers and cancellation are verified. Schema-29 encrypted context storage supports same-job reopen, denies foreign/JOBCTL access, authenticates ciphertext, participates in wrapping inventory/rotation and releases with completion/recovery. Failed create/cleanup/rewrap/migration preserve prior state. Independent socket jobs verify cross-call use, foreign-token denial and signoff cleanup; the actual server recovers contexts before readiness. Writable scalar RPG %SUBST preserves bytes/tails, checks ranges and retains literal text. WRKAPILS discovers 27 adapters; the normalized compatibility catalog has 304 entries. C14 software cryptography/key-management acceptance is complete under docs/cryptography-apis.md and the release contract. The three broader C14 API/ABI/reference-suite items and general C09/ILE work remain open. Checklist is now 36 complete / 70 open. |

### Accepted legacy aliases

| Old spelling | Disposition |
|---|---|
| `RMVFM` | RMVM |
| `DSPMENU` | GO |
| `MSG` | DSPMSG |

### Corrected provisional spellings (not aliases)

| Old spelling | Disposition |
|---|---|
| `WRKCLASS` | WRKCLS |
| `EDTOTA` | EDTOBJAUT |
| `ENDPRTWTR` | ENDWTR |
| `RLS` | RLSSPLF |
| `SND` | SNDNETSPLF |
| `RCVFNET` | RCVNETF |
| `QRDVDTAQ` | QRCVDTAQ |
| `QMHSNDPGM` | QMHSNDPM |
| `QWCRTVUS` | QUSRTVUS |
| `QUSCHGDQ` | QMHQCDQ |
| `QUSRDTAT` | QMHQRDQD |
| `QWCRJOB` | QUSRJOBI |
| `QWCAACTJ` | QUSLJOB |
| `QJORJRNI` | QjoRetrieveJournalEntries |
| `QUSCRTDQ` | CRTDTAQ |
| `RSTSECDTA` | RSTUSRPRF + RSTAUT |
| `JRNPF` | STRJRNPF |
| `GSHJRN` | DSPJRN |
| `QFRSMSG` | QMHSNDPM |
| `QWCS` | CHGSYSVAL |
| `GRTLIBL` | RTVJOBA |
| `ENDPGMCLS` | ENDPGM |
| `SETONLR` | SETON LR (fixed RPG) or *INLR = *ON (free RPG) |

### Rejected planning placeholders

| Old spelling | Disposition |
|---|---|
| `MLT` | Explicitly rejected in WP8. |
| `EXEC` | Explicitly rejected in WP8. |
| `PY` | Use external program CALL or QSH. |
| `BLDC` | Use CRTBNDC for external C builds. |
| `QAFC` | Authority checks use the shared authority service; no API with this provisional name. |
| `QGPL` | Library name, not an API. |
| `PRC` | Use CALLPRC. |
| `QP0Z` | API family prefix; use the explicit Qp0zLprintf contract. |
| `DMPCLPGM` | Optional diagnostic intent is met by STRDBG/listings; no such required command. |

## Observed RPG opcode parser mappings

Recognition is not execution/semantic acceptance. Fixed and free forms can differ.
Unknown operations fail compilation with original source locations. Fixed operations
occupy the emulator's seven-column operation field; ON-ERROR and SNDPGMMSG are free-only.

| Spelling | Fixed parser | Free parser | IR opcode |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ADD` | Recognized | — | `Add` |
| `BEGSR` | Recognized | Recognized | `BegSr` |
| `BITOFF` | Recognized | Recognized | `BitOff` |
| `BITON` | Recognized | Recognized | `BitOn` |
| `CALL` | Recognized | Recognized | `Call` |
| `CALLB` | Recognized | Recognized | `CallB` |
| `CALLP` | Recognized | Recognized | `CallP` |

See [RPG specification](rpg-spec.md) and [DDS specification](dds-schema.md) for
current subset details. [Display DDS](display-files.md) has a strict compiler and panel runtime.
[UIM help](shared-help.md) and [command definitions](command-definitions.md) are implemented.
Broader API host contracts, printer DDS, logical-file DDS, and the
complete typed command parameter catalog remain planned. Current PF DDS recognizes
A/S/P/B/F/L/D/T/Z fields and VARLEN/ALWNULL/CCSID/TEXT/DESC keywords; strict rejection
of every unsupported keyword and independent record-codec fixtures remain open.
